Their ancestors survived by stripping abandoned factories, battlefields, bunkers, and ruined cities for anything that could still be used. Over generations, this became more than survival. Scrap became wealth, weapons became status, and the ruins of the old world became their inheritance.

The Scrap Kin possess little respect for the institutions that existed before the Awakening. To them, the old governments failed, the military failed, and humanity's grand civilization failed. Everything left behind is simply something waiting to be taken.

They are heavily armed by necessity and accustomed to fighting over salvage. Their settlements are often built from the remains of industrial facilities, fortified with wreckage and whatever machinery they can repair.

The Scrap Kin are not mindless savages. They are practical, ruthless, and fiercely independent. They will trade when profitable, cooperate when necessary, and betray either arrangement when the opportunity is worth more.

[ WARNING ] WARNING

==== WHAT IS THIS? ====

This is a Warning message. It is a non-critical notification generated by the game when files are missing, unexpected events occur, or minor glitches are detected. Warnings are normally harmless and you can usually ignore them.

This mod is attempting to load a graphical asset (like a texture or icon) in the background. RimWorld's engine requires all visual assets to be loaded on the main game thread during startup. This is a technical warning for the mod author and is generally harmless for players, but it may indicate unoptimized code.

This can typically be ignored. If you experience visual glitches or slow loading, mentioning this warning to the mod author can help them optimize their code in a future update.
